title: Bounded background swap-in diagnostics for the practical pilot
tool: Existing paired performance runner and bounded native Engine

The first cooled original-pack run finishes within its physical ceiling with nominal thermal state and no sustained competing CPU. Four whole-host swap-ins, 65,536 bytes on this Mac, and zero swap-outs nevertheless exclude its timing under the frozen strict policy. The campaign is interrupted during its second process; the owner drains that child and preserves both cells and the incomplete coordinator. No complete comparison or selected-subset speed result is reported.

The next prospective pilot policy allows at most 1 MiB of whole-host swap-ins over an entire process. It keeps every counter and explicitly reports the bytes. Any swap-out, a larger aggregate swap-in delta, missing or inconsistent recorded page size, backward counters, OS pressure, insufficient headroom, physical-ceiling breach, thermal/power exclusion or sustained CPU contention still refuses or excludes the relevant run. Request timers cannot accumulate separate allowances. Original and v2 policies remain strict, and the new policy cannot qualify a held-out statistical study.

This is a bounded measurement heuristic for tiny reads of previously swapped pages, not proof that these reads are free or attributable to another app. It is not an allocation allowance and does not change product memory management. The two-minute continuous quiet/nominal admission and five-minute admission deadline remain. Measurements cover controlled interactive bursts, not sustained-session thermal performance or zero paging. A later revision needs fresh prospective evidence; no partial throughput outcome was inspected before freezing this change.

All twenty-two runner tests pass. The two initial logs retain a new fixture mistake: the existing helper numbers its callbacks from one, so the mutation guarded by zero never applied. Correcting the callback index makes the unchanged exclusion and byte-accounting assertions exercise the intended inputs. Tests cover both host page sizes, the exact byte boundary, one page over, any swap-out, historical strict verdicts, missing metadata, whole-process aggregation, analysis diagnostics, pilot-only admission and actual child cleanup with the new policy marker.

The frozen v8 protocol is a400188f0ebc3e81f35f47aeff943c83d2e5219b529d4407a6fd2537d4d25a23. The native binary, helper closure, candidate weights, recipes, workloads, paired order, three repetitions and 14/22 GB ceilings remain unchanged. It is prepared and unexecuted at this capture. No pack is promoted or published. The earlier cooling protocol is preserved in [[sources/runs/2026/10/2026-10-05-practical-cooling-retry]].

Each following block contains zlib-compressed exact bytes with original size and SHA-256.
